- **silent-meetings**: Run meetings where the document does the presenting. Trigger on phrases like silent meeting, read-first meeting, pre-read doc, reading block, turn this deck into a memo, speaker notes for the pre-read, talk track page by… ([andrewluxem/silent-meetings](https://github.com/andrewluxem/silent-meetings/tree/HEAD/skills/silent-meetings))

- **silent-require-failure-in-electron-ai**: When Electron main-process AI features fail with "Offline" despite valid API keys, check whether the `openai` npm package is installed — a silent `require('openai')` inside try/catch returns null and masks the real cause ([milodule3-debug/aura-code](https://github.com/milodule3-debug/aura-code/tree/HEAD/.qwen/skills/en/silent-require-failure-in-electron-ai) · ★3)

- **silhouette-analysis-threshold-optimization**: Use when when you have a pre-computed hierarchical dendrogram from structural clustering (e.g., of LC-MS features based on m/z and retention time) and want to compare or validate the cluster assignments produced by a fixed constant-threshold method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-analysis-threshold-optimization) · 2 copies)
- **silhouette-ari-purity-metric-computation**: Use when benchmarking cell embeddings from paired multi-omics integration methods and you need comparable embedding (silhouette) and clustering (purity, ARI) scores across methods and datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-ari-purity-metric-computation))
- **silhouette-based-cluster-heterogeneity-evaluation**: Use when a Seurat object has broad Louvain clusters that may mask rare cell types, and you need a quantitative criterion (per-cell silhouette score distributions) to decide which clusters are heterogeneous enough to recluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-based-cluster-heterogeneity-evaluation))
- **silhouette-based-cluster-number-selection**: Use when running CHAI-AvgSim or CHAI-SNF on scRNAseq data and the true number of clusters is unknown, so _k for Spectral Clustering must be estimated from the integrated similarity matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/silhouette-based-cluster-number-selection))
- **silhouette-based-cluster-number-selection**: Use when performing unsupervised clustering of single-cell expression data and you need a quantitative, unbiased criterion (silhouette score) to pick K instead of fixing cluster resolution ad hoc. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-based-cluster-number-selection))
- **silhouette-based-cluster-quality-evaluation**: Use when SRIQ clustering output (e.g. ..._Clusters_Frequencies.pdf) lists multiple candidate solutions across 10 iterations and you must rank them by recurrence, QSS, and silhouette to pick a stable solution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-based-cluster-quality-evaluation))
- **silhouette-based-clustering-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when comparing dimension-reduction or batch-integration methods for scRNA-seq (e.g., corralm vs PCA vs Seurat on multi-batch data) and you need a quantitative cell-type-preservation vs batch-mixing trade-off metric on cell embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-based-clustering-benchmark-evaluation))
- **silhouette-based-clustering-evaluation**: Use when comparing imputed scRNA-seq expression matrices (e.g., Bfimpute vs scImpute/DrImpute/PBLR outputs) on datasets with known cell type labels and you need silhouette-based clustering quality metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-based-clustering-evaluation))
- **silhouette-based-clustering-validation**: Use when you need to quantify whether an engineered/derived cell feature representation (e.g., Spearman or Pearson correlation, cosine, Euclidean, autoencoder features) improves consistency with known cell-type labels in scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-based-clustering-validation))
- **silhouette-based-optimal-cluster-count-selection**: Use when a table of statistically significant features (e.g. MB-VIP permutation p-value &lt; 0.01) must be partitioned into correlation-based clusters and the cluster count k is unknown and must be chosen objectively. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/silhouette-based-optimal-cluster-count-selection))
- **silhouette-based-optimal-k-selection**: Use when running CHAI-AvgSim or CHAI-SNF on a SingleCellExperiment (e.g., baron_mouse_1) and the true number of clusters is unknown, so a data-driven _k must be selected before Spectral Clustering on the AvgSim_matrix or snf_matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/silhouette-based-optimal-k-selection))
- **silhouette-based-wordbook-size-selection**: Use when converting GPCR (or other protein) sequences into bag-of-words features and the number of words in the wordbook must be determined from the data via the weighted Silhouette Coefficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/silhouette-based-wordbook-size-selection))
- **silhouette-cluster-quality-assessment**: Use when comparing two clusterings of the same scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., 5% vs 10% cell subsamples processed by SCC or SSCC) and you need a numeric measure of clustering consistency and per-cell cluster quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-cluster-quality-assessment))
- **silhouette-cluster-separability-evaluation**: Use when you have annotated single-cell expression matrices (e.g. Jurkat-293T with CD3D/XIST marker annotations, Zeisel with nine major classes) in raw vs. imputed versions and need to assess whether imputation improves cell-type subpopulation separability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-cluster-separability-evaluation))
- **silhouette-cluster-separation-evaluation**: Use when comparing manifold projection methods (topoAE, VAE, AE, UMAP) on single-cell modalities and you need a class-separation metric for each low-dimensional embedding, alongside KL0.01/KL0.1/KL1 density scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-cluster-separation-evaluation--ada98a9a94291497))
- **silhouette-cluster-separation-evaluation**: Use when comparing single-cell denoising or feature-selection methods and a ground-truth cell-phenotype labeling exists, so cluster separation can be quantified as mean silhouette score per method and latent dimension. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-cluster-separation-evaluation--3f4d4f44d10feb5b))
- **silhouette-cluster-separation-metric**: Use when running hsslda::runHSS feature selection on single-cell marker data (e.g., CyTOF metabolic markers) and you want subset scoring based on cluster cohesion vs. separation of a priori class labels rather than euclidean distance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-cluster-separation-metric))
- **silhouette-cluster-validation**: Use when a pairwise spectral similarity/distance matrix exists (e.g. from MS2DeepScore or MS2/MS similarity) and you must choose how many k-medoids clusters to group LC-MS/MS features into, with no prior label or target K. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/silhouette-cluster-validation))
- **silhouette-clustering-agreement-scoring**: Use when simulated scRNA-seq data (e.g. Splatter two- or six-group, 200 genes, 2000 cells, with dropout) has ground-truth celltype labels and you must numerically judge whether a denoiser (DCA ZINB vs MSE autoencoder) recovers celltype structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-clustering-agreement-scoring))
- **silhouette-coefficient-cluster-evaluation**: Use when comparing two cell embeddings (e.g., OnClass pred_Y_all score-PCA vs Scanorama-integrated expression) on labeled scRNA-seq data and you need a per-dataset, statistically testable measure of cluster separation by cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-coefficient-cluster-evaluation))
- **silhouette-coefficient-computation**: Use when benchmarking batch-effect correction methods on scRNA-seq data with known cell-type labels and you need an unsupervised measure of corrected-data separation quality beyond adjusted Rand index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-coefficient-computation--3c293e65ee590e85))
- **silhouette-coefficient-computation**: Use when scRNA-seq clustering output must be validated against known cell-type labels, or when testing whether engineered features (e.g., Spearman-correlation features from sscClust) remain consistent across subsampling rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-coefficient-computation--6bf59fac23b2b8b7))
- **silhouette-coefficient-computation**: Use when you have 2D UMAP coordinates plus cell-type labels from gene-annotation-based KNN/Seurat clustering and want to test whether an alternative feature space (e.g., uTAR reads outside gene annotations) clusters cell types comparably to annotated gene expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-coefficient-computation--0f22abb4eddf5be5))
- **silhouette-coefficient-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a cluster-purity or clustering-quality metric (e.g., ROGUE) against silhouette coefficients on simulated or real scRNA-seq populations, especially to test robustness to sequencing-depth or cell-number differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-coefficient-evaluation))
- **silhouette-coefficient-signature-number-selection**: Decision support for choosing the number of de novo SBS/ID mutational signatures (K) when running RNMF denovoNMF on a cohort's somatic mutation count matrix and needing a stable, data-driven K rather than an arbitrary choice. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/silhouette-coefficient-signature-number-selection))
- **silhouette-evaluation-of-embeddings**: Use when comparing 2D embeddings of scRNA-seq data for rare cell type separation, where a silhouette index computed over 100+ replicated runs (e.g., EDGE mean 0.851 vs UMAP 0.801 vs t-SNE 0.838 on PBMC) is needed as an objective metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-evaluation-of-embeddings))
- **silhouette-index-cluster-evaluation**: Use when comparing 2D embeddings (e.g., EDGE, t-SNE, UMAP) of labeled single-cell RNA-seq data and you need a quantitative, replication-averaged measure of how compactly and separately each cell type is projected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-index-cluster-evaluation))
- **silhouette-index-clustering-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a scRNA-seq feature selection or clustering method and you need a label-based quantitative score of cluster separation across feature set sizes (e.g., 50-4000 genes). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-index-clustering-evaluation--3e366b81ee81bba5))
- **silhouette-index-clustering-evaluation**: Use when comparing dimension-reduction or visualization methods for scRNA-seq data and you need a label-referenced metric of cell-type separation for each 2D embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-index-clustering-evaluation--2e008f360243c5d8))
- **silhouette-score**: Compute the silhouette_score metric — provided by scikit-learn. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/silhouette-score) · ★3)
- **silhouette-score-cell-type-evaluation**: Use when comparing dimensionality-reduction or VAE embeddings of scRNA-seq data with known cell-type labels and you need a quantitative, per-method separation metric to supplement visual checks for crowding or overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-score-cell-type-evaluation))
- **silhouette-score-cluster-evaluation**: Use when selecting unsupervised segmentation parameters (e.g., t-SNE perplexity, learning rate, iteration count) or comparing search strategies, and an internal cluster-quality metric is needed to rank k-means segmentations of MSI pixel data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/silhouette-score-cluster-evaluation))
- **silhouette-score-cluster-structure-evaluation**: Use when you have per-sequence embedding vectors from a trained classifier (here: 69,335 Greengenes2 2024.09 test sequences) plus taxonomy labels, and you need to test whether the embedding space clusters by taxonomic rank. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/silhouette-score-cluster-structure-evaluation))
- **silhouette-score-cluster-validation**: Use when clustering tabular embeddings (e.g., 100-d Word2Vec vectors of FASTA files) with scikit-learn KMeans and you need a single numeric metric to verify the cluster assignment reproduces a reported reference score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/silhouette-score-cluster-validation))
- **silhouette-score-clustering-evaluation**: Use when you need to quantitatively compare clustering performance of DR methods (e.g., scLENS vs Seurat/Scanpy PCs) on single-cell datasets with known ground-truth labels across varying sparsity or TGC variability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-score-clustering-evaluation))
- **silhouette-score-computation**: Use when a consensus partitioning run (e.g. cola over multiple k values) needs per-sample or per-k stability assessment, or when ambiguous samples must be excluded before PAC calculation or signature analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-score-computation))
- **silhouette-score-computation**: Use when comparing cross-platform scRNA-seq integration quality — e.g. after Seurat integration of platform-specific gene-by-barcode matrices (GBMs) with a reference GBM — by scoring cluster separation alongside kBET. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-score-computation))
- **silhouette-score-distance-matrix-validation**: Use when a patient/specimen-level distance matrix (Wasserstein/EMD between cell-cluster distributions) has been computed and you need an unsupervised quality metric of that matrix, or when benchmarking distance methods with ARI/Silhouette/AUCPR/Spearman metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-score-distance-matrix-validation))
- **silhouette-score-embedding-assessment**: Use when a joint embedding of annotated scRNA-seq and unlabeled scATAC-seq cells (e.g., scBridge output or a corrupted/downsampled source run) needs a quantitative embedding-quality metric alongside label transfer accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-score-embedding-assessment))
- **silhouette-score-evaluation**: Use when a Seurat/SCISSORS clustering must be assessed for heterogeneity, when comparing clusterings across methods with violin plots of per-cell silhouettes, or when deciding which broad clusters to recluster for rare subpopulations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-score-evaluation))
- **silhouette-score-evaluation-of-cell-type-clustering**: Use when comparing scRNA-seq integration/annotation pipelines (e.g., 16 MASI variants of raw vs. PlinerScore matrices, marker vs. HVG features) and you need a numeric, statistically testable measure of whether cell-type structure is resolved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-score-evaluation-of-cell-type-clustering))
- **silhouette-score-heterogeneity-assessment**: Use when you have preprocessed bulk RNA-seq data for a cohort (e.g., &lt; 100 samples) and need to decide how many representative clusters exist so that all representatives can be selected in one batch instead of multi-round active learning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-score-heterogeneity-assessment))
- **silhouette-score-k-selection**: Use when running CHAI-AvgSim or CHAI-SNF on scRNAseq data where the true number of clusters is unknown and a _k must be selected for Spectral Clustering via silhouette score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/silhouette-score-k-selection))
- **silhouette-significance-testing**: Use when validating joint clustering/embedding of paired scRNA-seq and scATAC-seq against surrogate ground truth labels from bulk data, e.g. comparing CoupledNMF joint t-SNE to Cell Ranger or Seurat V3 label transfer via silhouette index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-significance-testing))
- **silhouette-width-batch-mixing-analysis**: Use when comparing imputation/denoising methods on multi-batch scRNA-Seq data to quantify batch-effect removal while confirming cell-type separation, e.g., merging batches of the same cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-width-batch-mixing-analysis))
- **silhouette-width-celltype-assessment**: Use when evaluating a batch-corrected/embedded Seurat object (e.g. STACAS output) to determine whether cell type structure survives integration, or when demonstrating that batch-mixing metrics like iLISI reward overcorrected embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-width-celltype-assessment))
- **silhouette-width-cluster-label-validation**: Use when you have external cluster labels (e.g., from a different modality or a GMM fit) and need to quantify how well-separated those clusters are in an expression matrix, such as testing whether denoising recovers structure lost to dropout. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-width-cluster-label-validation))
- **silhouette-width-cluster-separation-analysis**: Use when you need to validate that a learned feature space (e.g. ANN deep features) yields more compact within-group and better separated between-group clusters than a baseline feature space on the same labeled samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-width-cluster-separation-analysis))
- **silhouette-width-cluster-separation-assessment**: Use when comparing low-dimensional representations (e.g., PCA vs ZIFA vs ZINB-WaVE) of scRNA-seq data for a fixed set of known cell-type labels, and you need a single cluster-separation score per embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-width-cluster-separation-assessment))
- **silhouette-width-evaluation**: Use when a droplet-based scRNA-seq clustering has produced per-cell cluster memberships (e.g., the mem output of BAMM-SC) and you must report cluster tightness as average silhouette width, and cluster stability as APN under gene-perturbation reruns, to compare against competing methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-width-evaluation))
- **silhouette-width-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating single-cell integration embeddings (e.g. stored in adata.obsm['delta']) for whether cell-type structure is preserved (ASW) and whether batches are over- or under-corrected (ASW-beta). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/silhouette-width-metric-computation))
